.PHONY: migrate-create\
migrate-create:\
	@if [ -z "$(MSG)" ]; then \\
		echo "âƒ½ƒ¬ Please provide a migration message: make migrate-create MSG='add user table'"; \\
		exit 1; \\
	fi\
	@echo "ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ½ Creating migration: $(MSG)"\
	$(UV) run alembic revision --autogenerate -m "$(MSG)"\
	@echo "âƒ¼ƒ¥ Migration created. Review it with 'alembic upgrade head'."\

.PHONY: clean\
clean:\
	@echo "ðƒ¿§¹ Cleaning cache and build artifacts..."\
	find . -type d -name "__pycache__" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true\
	find . -type d -name "*.egg-info" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true\
	find . -type d -name ".pytest_cache" -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true\
	rm -rf .coverage htmlcov build dist 2>/dev/null || true\
	@echo "âƒ¼ƒ¥ Clean complete."\
\

cat > Dockerfile << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Backend Dockerfile\
# ============================================================\
# Multi-stage build for small image size\
# ============================================================\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Builder Stage âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
FROM python:3.11-slim-bookworm AS builder\
\
WORKDIR /app\
\
# Install system dependencies required for some packages\
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \\
    gcc \\
    libpq-dev \\
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\
\
# Install uv (fast Python package installer)\
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir uv\
\
# Copy dependency files first (for better caching)\
COPY pyproject.toml uv.lock ./\
\
# Create virtual environment and install dependencies\
RUN uv venv /opt/venv && \\
    . /opt/venv/bin/activate && \\
    uv sync --frozen --no-dev --extra monitoring\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Runtime Stage âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
FROM python:3.11-slim-bookworm AS runtime\
\
WORKDIR /app\
\
# Copy virtual environment from builder\
COPY --from=builder /opt/venv /opt/venv\
\
# Copy application source code\
COPY src/ ./src/\
COPY alembic.ini ./alembic.ini\
COPY migrations/ ./migrations/\
\
# Ensure the virtual environment is used\
ENV PATH="/opt/venv/bin:$PATH"\
ENV PYTHONPATH="/app:$PYTHONPATH"\
\
# Health check\
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \\
    CMD python -c "import urllib.request; urllib.request.urlopen('http://localhost:8000/health')" || exit 1\
\
# Expose the application port\
EXPOSE 8000\
\
# Run the application\
CMD ["uvicorn", "src.app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]\
EOF
cat > docker-compose.yml << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Full Stack Docker Compose\
# ============================================================\
# Services:\
#   - PostgreSQL 16 + pgvector (vector database)\
#   - Redis 7 (caching / rate limiting)\
#   - Backend (FastAPI + LangGraph)\
#   - Frontend (ChatGPT-Next-Web)\
#   - Grafana (optional observability)\
# ============================================================\
\
services:\
  # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ PostgreSQL + pgvector âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
  postgres:\
    image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16\
    container_name: rag_postgres\
    environment:\
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres\
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres\
      POSTGRES_DB: rag\
    ports:\
      - "5432:5432"\
    volumes:\
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data\
    healthcheck:\
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U postgres -d rag"]\
      interval: 10s\
      timeout: 5s\
      retries: 5\
    restart: unless-stopped\
    networks:\
      - rag_network\
    logging:\
      driver: "json-file"\
      options:\
        max-size: "10m"\
        max-file: "3"\
\
  # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Redis âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
  redis:\
    image: redis:7-alpine\
    container_name: rag_redis\
    ports:\
      - "6379:6379"\
    volumes:\
      - redis_data:/data\
    healthcheck:\
      test: ["CMD", "redis-cli", "ping"]\
      interval: 10s\
      timeout: 5s\

  # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Backend (Our FastAPI App) âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
  backend:\
    build:\
      context: .\
      dockerfile: Dockerfile\
    container_name: rag_backend\
    environment:\
      # Database\
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://REDACTED
      # Redis\
      REDIS_URL: redis://REDACTED
      # LLM (Local vLLM or OpenRouter â€ƒ´ placeholder)\

      ENVIRONMENT: development\
      LOG_LEVEL: info\
    ports:\
      - "8000:8000"\
    depends_on:\
      postgres:\
        condition: service_healthy\
      redis:\
        condition: service_healthy\

      - ./src:/app/src  # Mount source for hot-reload during development\
      - ./migrations:/app/migrations\
    restart: unless-stopped\
    networks:\
      - rag_network\
    extra_hosts:\
      - "host.docker.internal:host-gateway"  # Allows access to host's local LLM\

      driver: "json-file"\
      options:\
        max-size: "10m"\
        max-file: "3"\
\

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Full Stack Docker Compose\
# ============================================================\
# Services:\
#   - PostgreSQL 16 + pgvector (vector database)\
#   - Redis 7 (caching / rate limiting)\
#   - Backend (FastAPI + LangGraph) â€ƒ´ port 8080\
#   - Frontend (ChatGPT-Next-Web)   â€ƒ´ port 8081\
#   - Grafana (optional)            â€ƒ´ port 8082\
#   - pgAdmin (optional)            â€ƒ´ port 8083\
# ============================================================\
\
services:\
  # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ PostgreSQL + pgvector âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
  postgres:\
    image: pgvector/pgvector:pg16\
    container_name: rag_postgres\
    environment:\
      POSTGRES_USER: postgres\
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: postgres\
      POSTGRES_DB: rag\
    ports:\

cat > Dockerfile << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Backend Dockerfile\
# ============================================================\
# Multi-stage build for small image size\
# ============================================================\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Builder Stage âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
FROM python:3.11-slim-bookworm AS builder\
\
WORKDIR /app\
\
# Install system dependencies\
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \\
    gcc \\
    libpq-dev \\
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\
\
# Install uv\
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir uv\
\
# Copy dependency files first (for better caching)\
COPY pyproject.toml uv.lock ./\
\
# Create virtual environment and install dependencies\
RUN uv venv /opt/venv && \\
    . /opt/venv/bin/activate && \\
    uv sync --frozen --no-dev --extra monitoring\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Runtime Stage âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
FROM python:3.11-slim-bookworm AS runtime\
\
WORKDIR /app\
\
# Copy virtual environment from builder\
COPY --from=builder /opt/venv /opt/venv\
\
# Copy application source code\
COPY src/ ./src/\
\
# Ensure the virtual environment is used\
ENV PATH="/opt/venv/bin:$PATH"\
ENV PYTHONPATH="/app:$PYTHONPATH"\
\
# Health check\
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=10s --start-period=5s --retries=3 \\
    CMD python -c "import urllib.request; urllib.request.urlopen('http://localhost:8000/health')" || exit 1\
\
# Expose the application port\
EXPOSE 8000\
\
# Run the application\
CMD ["uvicorn", "src.app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]\
EOF
# Build the backend image (the frontend image is already pulled)\
docker-compose build\
\
# Start all services\
make docker-up
# Remove the old backend image\
docker rmi work_credit-rag_phase1-backend --force 2>/dev/null || true\
\
# Rebuild with no cache\
docker-compose build --no-cache backend
make docker-up

\

\

EOF

\

cat > Dockerfile << 'EOF'\
FROM python:3.11-slim\
\
WORKDIR /app\
\
# Install system dependencies (cached)\
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \\
    gcc \\
    libpq-dev \\
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\
\
# Copy only dependency files first (to leverage caching)\
COPY pyproject.toml uv.lock ./\
\
# Install uv and dependencies (this layer is cached unless pyproject.toml changes)\
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir uv && \\
    uv pip install --system -e ".[monitoring]"\
\
# Copy the actual source code (this layer is rebuilt on every code change)\
COPY src/ ./src/\
\
EXPOSE 8000\
\
CMD ["uvicorn", "src.app.main:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]\
EOF
# Remove the old failed image\
docker rmi work_credit-rag_phase1-backend --force 2>/dev/null || true\
\

# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ FastAPI App âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\

)\

# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ CORS âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\

# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Health Check âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@app.get("/health")\
async def health_check():\

\

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Configuration\
# ============================================================\
\
import secrets\
from typing import Optional\
\
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings\
\
\
class Settings(BaseSettings):\
    PROJECT_NAME: str = "RAG Agent Platform"\
    ENVIRONMENT: str = "development"\
    DEBUG: bool = True\
    LOG_LEVEL: str = "info"\
\
    # LLM\
    OPENAI_API_KEY: str\
    OPENAI_BASE_URL: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1"\
    OPENAI_MODEL: str = "gpt-4o-mini"\
\
    # Database\
    DATABASE_URL: str\
\
    # Redis\
    REDIS_URL: str = "redis://REDACTED
\
    # Security\
    ACCESS_CODE: Optional[str] = None\
    ACCESS_CODE_SEED: Optional[str] = None\
    SUPER_SECRET_KEY: str = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\
\
    # Rate limiting\
    RATE_LIMIT_REQUESTS: int = 100\
    RATE_LIMIT_PERIOD: int = 60\
\
    # Monitoring\
    LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY: Optional[str] = None\
    LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY: Optional[str] = None\
    LANGFUSE_HOST: str = "https://cloud.langfuse.com"\
\
    # Vector\
    VECTOR_DIMENSION: int = 1536\
    EMBEDDING_MODEL: str = "text-embedding-3-small"\
\
    class Config:\
        env_file = ".env"\
        env_file_encoding = "utf-8"\
\
\
settings = Settings()\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Security Module\

import hashlib\
import secrets\

from typing import Optional\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ ASCII ART BANNER âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
ASCII_BANNER = r"""\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨       âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨      âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨      âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
     âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
"""\
\
BORDER = "=" * 80\
\
\
def generate_access_code(seed: Optional[str] = None) -> tuple[str, str]:\
    """Generate access code from ASCII art + seed + date + salt."""\
    if seed is None:\
        seed = settings.ACCESS_CODE_SEED or secrets.token_urlsafe(16)\
\
    today = datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")\
    salt = "RAG_PLATFORM_v1.0_SALT"\
\
    raw = f"{ASCII_BANNER.strip()}:{seed}:{today}:{salt}"\
    checksum = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()\
    code = f"rag-{checksum[:8]}-{seed[:8]}"\
    return code, seed\
\
\
def print_startup_banner() -> str:\
    """Print the ASCII banner with access code and return the code."""\
    if settings.ACCESS_CODE:\
        code = settings.ACCESS_CODE\
        seed = settings.ACCESS_CODE_SEED or "manual"\
    else:\
        code, seed = generate_access_code()\
        # Optionally save to settings (but it's runtime only)\
\
    banner = f"""\
{BORDER}\
{ASCII_BANNER}\
{BORDER}\
ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ°  ACCESS CODE:  {code}\
ðƒ¿§‚  SEED:         {seed}\
ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ¥  DATE:         {datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")}\
ðƒ¿ƒ¬ƒ°  FRONTEND:     http://localhost:8081\
{BORDER}\
\
ðƒ¿ƒ²ƒ  Use this access code to log into the chat interface.\
"""\
    print(banner)\
    return code\
\
\
if __name__ == "__main__":\
    print_startup_banner()\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ FastAPI Application Entry Point\
# ============================================================\
\
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager\
\
from fastapi import FastAPI\
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\
\
from app.api.router import api_router\
from app.core.security import print_startup_banner\
\
\
@asynccontextmanager\
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\
    """Startup and shutdown events."""\
    # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Startup âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
    access_code = print_startup_banner()\
    # You can store access_code in app.state if needed\
    yield\
    # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Shutdown âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
    print("ðƒ¿ƒ»ƒ± Shutting down...")\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ FastAPI App âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
app = FastAPI(\
    title="RAG Agent Platform",\
    version="0.1.0",\
    description="Production-ready RAG agent with LangGraph, pgvector, and FastAPI",\
    lifespan=lifespan,\
)\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ CORS âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
app.add_middleware(\
    CORSMiddleware,\
    allow_origins=["*"],\
    allow_credentials=True,\
    allow_methods=["*"],\
    allow_headers=["*"],\
)\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Include Routers âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
app.include_router(api_router, prefix="")  # Mount at root\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Health Check âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@app.get("/health")\
async def health_check():\
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "RAG Agent Platform"}\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Root âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@app.get("/")\
async def root():\
    return {\
        "message": "RAG Agent Platform API",\
        "docs": "/docs",\
        "health": "/health",\
    }\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ OpenAI-Compatible Chat Endpoint\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import List, Optional\
\
from fastapi import APIRouter\
from pydantic import BaseModel\
\
router = APIRouter()\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Request/Response Models âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
class ChatMessage(BaseModel):\
    role: str  # "user", "assistant", "system"\
    content: str\
\
class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):\
    model: str = "rag-agent"\
    messages: List[ChatMessage]\
    stream: bool = False\
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7\
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048\
\
class Choice(BaseModel):\
    index: int\
    message: ChatMessage\
    finish_reason: str\
\
class ChatCompletionResponse(BaseModel):\
    id: str\
    object: str\
    created: int\
    model: str\
    choices: List[Choice]\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Endpoint âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@router.post("/v1/chat/completions")\
async def chat_completions(request: ChatCompletionRequest):\
    """\
    OpenAI-compatible chat completion endpoint.\
    Currently echoes the user's last message as a demo.\
    """\
    # Extract the last user message\
    user_message = None\
    for msg in reversed(request.messages):\
        if msg.role == "user":\
            user_message = msg.content\
            break\
\
    if not user_message:\
        user_message = "No user message found."\
\
    # Simple echo response (replace with LangGraph agent later)\
    assistant_message = f"Echo: {user_message}"\
\
    # Build response\
    response = ChatCompletionResponse(\
        id="chatcmpl-echo",\
        object="chat.completion",\
        created=1234567890,\
        model=request.model,\
        choices=[\
            Choice(\
                index=0,\
                message=ChatMessage(role="assistant", content=assistant_message),\
                finish_reason="stop"\
            )\
        ]\
    )\
\
    return response\
EOF

app.include_router(api_router, prefix="")  # Mount at root\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Health Check âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@app.get("/health")\
async def health_check():\
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "RAG Agent Platform"}\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Root âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\

async def root():\
    return {\
        "message": "RAG Agent Platform API",\
        "docs": "/docs",\
        "health": "/health",\
    }\

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Security Module\
# ============================================================\
\
import hashlib\
import secrets\
import sys\
from datetime import datetime\
from typing import Optional\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ ASCII ART BANNER âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
ASCII_BANNER = r"""\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨       âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨      âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨      âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
     âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨  âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨ âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨   âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨    âƒ¶ƒ¨âƒ¶ƒ¨\
"""\
\
BORDER = "=" * 80\
\
\
def generate_access_code(seed: Optional[str] = None) -> tuple[str, str]:\
    """Generate access code from ASCII art + seed + date + salt."""\
    if seed is None:\
        seed = settings.ACCESS_CODE_SEED or secrets.token_urlsafe(16)\
\
    today = datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")\
    salt = "RAG_PLATFORM_v1.0_SALT"\
\
    raw = f"{ASCII_BANNER.strip()}:{seed}:{today}:{salt}"\
    checksum = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()\
    code = f"rag-{checksum[:8]}-{seed[:8]}"\
    return code, seed\
\
\
def print_startup_banner() -> str:\
    """Print the ASCII banner with access code and return the code."""\
    if settings.ACCESS_CODE:\
        code = settings.ACCESS_CODE\
        seed = settings.ACCESS_CODE_SEED or "manual"\
    else:\
        code, seed = generate_access_code()\
\
    banner = f"""\
{BORDER}\
{ASCII_BANNER}\
{BORDER}\
ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ°  ACCESS CODE:  {code}\
ðƒ¿§‚  SEED:         {seed}\
ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ¥  DATE:         {datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC")}\
ðƒ¿ƒ¬ƒ°  FRONTEND:     http://localhost:8081\
{BORDER}\
\
ðƒ¿ƒ²ƒ  Use this access code to log into the chat interface.\
"""\
    sys.stdout.write(banner)\
    sys.stdout.flush()\
    return code\
\
\
if __name__ == "__main__":\
    print_startup_banner()\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ FastAPI Application Entry Point\
# ============================================================\
\
import sys\
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager\
\
from fastapi import FastAPI\
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\
\
from app.api.router import api_router\
from app.core.security import print_startup_banner\
\
\
@asynccontextmanager\
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\
    """Startup and shutdown events."""\
    # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Startup âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
    sys.stdout.write("ðƒ¿ƒº€ Starting lifespan...\n")\
    sys.stdout.flush()\
    access_code = print_startup_banner()\
    # Store the code for later use if needed\
    app.state.access_code = access_code\
    yield\
    # âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Shutdown âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
    sys.stdout.write("ðƒ¿ƒ»ƒ± Shutting down...\n")\
    sys.stdout.flush()\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ FastAPI App âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
app = FastAPI(\
    title="RAG Agent Platform",\
    version="0.1.0",\
    description="Production-ready RAG agent with LangGraph, pgvector, and FastAPI",\
    lifespan=lifespan,\
)\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ CORS âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
app.add_middleware(\
    CORSMiddleware,\
    allow_origins=["*"],\
    allow_credentials=True,\
    allow_methods=["*"],\
    allow_headers=["*"],\
)\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Include Routers âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
app.include_router(api_router, prefix="")  # Mount at root\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Health Check âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@app.get("/health")\
async def health_check():\
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "RAG Agent Platform"}\
\

async def root():\

cat > src/app/api/v1/chat.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ OpenAI-Compatible Chat Endpoint\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import List, Optional\
\
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException\
from pydantic import BaseModel\
import httpx\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
\
router = APIRouter()\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Request/Response Models âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
class ChatMessage(BaseModel):\
    role: str  # "user", "assistant", "system"\
    content: str\
\
class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):\
    model: str = "gpt-4o-mini"\
    messages: List[ChatMessage]\
    stream: bool = False\
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7\
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048\
\
class Choice(BaseModel):\
    index: int\
    message: ChatMessage\
    finish_reason: str\
\
class Usage(BaseModel):\
    prompt_tokens: int\
    completion_tokens: int\
    total_tokens: int\
\
class ChatCompletionResponse(BaseModel):\
    id: str\
    object: str\
    created: int\
    model: str\
    choices: List[Choice]\
    usage: Optional[Usage] = None\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Endpoint âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@router.post("/v1/chat/completions")\
async def chat_completions(request: ChatCompletionRequest):\
    """\
    OpenAI-compatible chat completion endpoint.\
    Forwards requests to OpenAI API.\
    """\
    # Prepare OpenAI API request\
    headers = {\
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENAI_API_KEY}",\
        "Content-Type": "application/json",\
    }\
\
    # Build the request body for OpenAI\
    openai_payload = {\
        "model": request.model,\
        "messages": [msg.dict() for msg in request.messages],\
        "temperature": request.temperature,\
        "max_tokens": request.max_tokens,\
    }\
\
    # Add stream if requested\
    if request.stream:\
        openai_payload["stream"] = True\
\
    try:\
        # Make the request to OpenAI\
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:\
            response = await client.post(\
                f"{settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions",\
                headers=headers,\
                json=openai_payload,\
            )\
\
        # Check for errors\
        if response.status_code != 200:\
            error_detail = response.text\
            raise HTTPException(\
                status_code=response.status_code,\
                detail=f"OpenAI API error: {error_detail}"\
            )\
\
        # Parse the response\
        openai_response = response.json()\
\
        # Build our response\
        choices = []\
        for idx, choice in enumerate(openai_response.get("choices", [])):\
            choices.append(\
                Choice(\
                    index=idx,\
                    message=ChatMessage(\
                        role=choice.get("message", {}).get("role", "assistant"),\
                        content=choice.get("message", {}).get("content", ""),\
                    ),\
                    finish_reason=choice.get("finish_reason", "stop"),\
                )\
            )\
\
        usage = None\
        if "usage" in openai_response:\
            usage = Usage(\
                prompt_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("prompt_tokens", 0),\
                completion_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("completion_tokens", 0),\
                total_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("total_tokens", 0),\
            )\
\
        return ChatCompletionResponse(\
            id=openai_response.get("id", "chatcmpl-unknown"),\
            object=openai_response.get("object", "chat.completion"),\
            created=openai_response.get("created", 1234567890),\
            model=openai_response.get("model", request.model),\
            choices=choices,\
            usage=usage,\
        )\
\
    except httpx.TimeoutException:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=504,\
            detail="OpenAI API request timed out"\
        )\
    except Exception as e:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=500,\
            detail=f"Error calling OpenAI API: {str(e)}"\
        )\
EOF

# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Configuration âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\

def print_header():\
    """Print the test header with ASCII art."""\
    header = """\
âƒµƒ´âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ·\

"""\
    console.print(Panel(header, style="bold blue", box=box.DOUBLE))\
\
\
def test_api_key() -> bool:\
    """Test if the API key is valid by listing models."""\
    with Progress(\
        SpinnerColumn(),\
        TextColumn("[progress.description]{task.description}"),\
        console=console,\
    ) as progress:\
        task = progress.add_task("[cyan]Testing API key validity...", total=None)\
\
        try:\
            headers = {\
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {OPENAI_API_KEY}",\
                "Content-Type": "application/json",\
            }\
\
            with httpx.Client(timeout=10.0) as client:\
                response = client.get(\
                    f"{OPENAI_BASE_URL}/models",\
                    headers=headers,\
                )\
\
            progress.update(task, completed=True)\
\
            if response.status_code == 200:\
                console.print("âƒ¼ƒ¥ [green]API key is valid[/green]")\
                data = response.json()\
                model_count = len(data.get("data", []))\
                console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒª Available models: [bold]{model_count}[/bold]")\
                return True\
            elif response.status_code == 401:\
                console.print("âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Invalid API key[/red]")\
                console.print(f"   Status: {response.status_code}")\
                console.print(f"   Detail: {response.text}")\
                return False\
            else:\
                console.print(f"âƒºƒ€ï¸ƒ¯  [yellow]Unexpected response: {response.status_code}[/yellow]")\
                console.print(f"   Detail: {response.text}")\
                return False\
\
        except httpx.TimeoutException:\
            console.print("âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Connection timeout - API endpoint unreachable[/red]")\
            return False\
        except Exception as e:\
            console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Error: {str(e)}[/red]")\
            return False\
\
\
def test_chat_completion() -> Optional[dict]:\
    """Test a simple chat completion."""\
    with Progress(\
        SpinnerColumn(),\
        TextColumn("[progress.description]{task.description}"),\
        console=console,\
    ) as progress:\
        task = progress.add_task("[cyan]Testing chat completion...", total=None)\
\
        try:\
            headers = {\
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {OPENAI_API_KEY}",\
                "Content-Type": "application/json",\
            }\
\
            payload = {\
                "model": TEST_MODEL,\
                "messages": [\
                    {"role": "user", "content": "Reply with exactly 'OK' if you can read this."}\
                ],\
                "max_tokens": 10,\
                "temperature": 0,\
            }\
\
            start_time = time.time()\
\
            with httpx.Client(timeout=30.0) as client:\
                response = client.post(\
                    f"{OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions",\
                    headers=headers,\
                    json=payload,\
                )\
\
            elapsed = time.time() - start_time\
\
            progress.update(task, completed=True)\
\
            if response.status_code == 200:\
                data = response.json()\
                console.print("âƒ¼ƒ¥ [green]Chat completion successful[/green]")\
                console.print(f"   âƒ¯±ï¸ƒ¯  Response time: [bold]{elapsed:.2f}[/bold] seconds")\
\
                # Extract the response\
                choices = data.get("choices", [])\
                if choices:\
                    message = choices[0].get("message", {})\
                    content = message.get("content", "")\
                    console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ²¬ Response: [italic]\"{content}\"[/italic]")\
\
                # Usage info\
                usage = data.get("usage", {})\
                if usage:\
                    console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ½ Tokens used: [bold]{usage.get('total_tokens', 0)}[/bold]")\
                    console.print(f"      Prompt: {usage.get('prompt_tokens', 0)} | "\
                                f"Completion: {usage.get('completion_tokens', 0)}")\
\
                return data\
            else:\
                console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Chat completion failed: {response.status_code}[/red]")\
                console.print(f"   Detail: {response.text}")\
                return None\
\
        except httpx.TimeoutException:\
            console.print("âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Request timeout - API took too long to respond[/red]")\
            return None\
        except Exception as e:\
            console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Error: {str(e)}[/red]")\
            return None\
\
\
def test_connection_details():\
    """Test and display connection details."""\
    table = Table(title="Connection Details", box=box.ROUNDED)\
    table.add_column("Property", style="cyan", no_wrap=True)\
    table.add_column("Value", style="white")\
\
    table.add_row("API Base URL", OPENAI_BASE_URL)\
    table.add_row("Test Model", TEST_MODEL)\
    table.add_row("API Key Length", f"{len(OPENAI_API_KEY)} characters")\
    table.add_row("API Key Prefix", f"{OPENAI_API_KEY[:10]}...")\
    table.add_row("Timestamp", datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC"))\
\
    console.print(table)\
\
\
def main():\
    """Run all tests."""\
    print_header()\
\
    # Connection details\
    test_connection_details()\
    console.print()\
\
    # Test API key\
    console.print("[bold]ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ± Testing API Credentials[/bold]")\
    console.print("âƒ´€" * 60)\
    is_valid = test_api_key()\
    console.print()\
\
    if not is_valid:\
        console.print("[red]âƒ½ƒ¬ API key validation failed. Please check your key.[/red]")\
        sys.exit(1)\
\
    # Test chat completion\
    console.print("[bold]ðƒ¿ƒ²¬ Testing Chat Completion[/bold]")\
    console.print("âƒ´€" * 60)\
    result = test_chat_completion()\
    console.print()\
\
    if result:\
        console.print("[green]âƒ¼ƒ¥ All tests passed![/green]")\
        console.print("[dim]Your OpenAI API key is working correctly.[/dim]")\
        console.print()\
        console.print("[bold]ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ« Summary:[/bold]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ API Key: [green]Valid[/green]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ Connection: [green]Successful[/green]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ Model: [green]{TEST_MODEL}[/green]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ Response: [green]Received[/green]")\
        sys.exit(0)\
    else:\
        console.print("[red]âƒ½ƒ¬ Tests failed. Please check your configuration.[/red]")\
        sys.exit(1)\
\
\
if __name__ == "__main__":\
    main()\
EOF

cat > src/app/core/secrets.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Secure API Key Management\
# ============================================================\
# Never expose keys in code, logs, or error messages.\
# Keys are loaded from environment variables only.\
# ============================================================\
\
import os\
import re\
from typing import Optional\
from functools import lru_cache\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
\
\
class SecretManager:\
    """Secure API key management with validation and redaction."""\
\
    @staticmethod\
    def get_openai_key() -> str:\
        """\
        Get OpenAI API key from environment.\
        Raises ValueError if key is missing or invalid format.\
        """\
        key = settings.OPENAI_API_KEY\
\
        if not key or key == "your-secret-key-here":\
            raise ValueError(\
                "OPENAI_API_KEY not set. Please add it to your .env file."\
            )\
\
        # Validate format (sk-REDACTED or sk-)\
        if not re.match(r"^sk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$", key):\
            raise ValueError(\
                "OPENAI_API_KEY appears to be invalid format. "\
                "It should start with 'sk-' or 'sk-REDACTED'."\
            )\
\
        return key\
\
    @staticmethod\
    def get_redacted_key() -> str:\
        """Get a redacted version of the API key for logging."""\
        try:\
            key = SecretManager.get_openai_key()\
            if len(key) > 16:\
                return f"{key[:8]}...{key[-8:]}"\
            return "***"\
        except ValueError:\
            return "[NOT SET]"\
\
    @staticmethod\
    def validate_all_keys() -> dict:\
        """Validate all required API keys and return status."""\
        status = {}\
\
        # OpenAI\
        try:\
            SecretManager.get_openai_key()\
            status["openai"] = {"status": "valid", "redacted": SecretManager.get_redacted_key()}\
        except ValueError as e:\
            status["openai"] = {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}\
\
        return status\
\
\
@lru_cache(maxsize=1)\
def get_openai_api_key() -> str:\
    """Cached version of the API key getter."""\
    return SecretManager.get_openai_key()\
\
\
def redact_sensitive_data(text: str) -> str:\
    """Redact any sensitive data from logs or error messages."""\
    # Redact OpenAI keys\
    text = re.sub(\
        r"sk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}",\
        "[REDACTED_API_KEY]",\
        text\
    )\
    # Redact Bearer tokens\
    text = re.sub(\
        r"Bearer\s+sk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}",\
        "Bearer [REDACTED_API_KEY]",\
        text\
    )\
    return text\
\
\
# Convenience exports\
openai_key = get_openai_api_key\
redacted_key = SecretManager.get_redacted_key\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Configuration\
# ============================================================\
\
import secrets\
from typing import Optional\
\
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings\
\
\
class Settings(BaseSettings):\
    PROJECT_NAME: str = "RAG Agent Platform"\
    ENVIRONMENT: str = "development"\
    DEBUG: bool = True\
    LOG_LEVEL: str = "info"\
\
    # LLM - NEVER log these values\
    OPENAI_API_KEY: str = ""\
    OPENAI_BASE_URL: str = "https://api.openai.com/v1"\
    OPENAI_MODEL: str = "gpt-4o-mini"\
\
    # Database\
    DATABASE_URL: str = "postgresql://REDACTED
\
    # Redis\
    REDIS_URL: str = "redis://REDACTED
\
    # Security\
    ACCESS_CODE: Optional[str] = None\
    ACCESS_CODE_SEED: Optional[str] = None\
    SUPER_SECRET_KEY: str = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\
\
    # Rate limiting\
    RATE_LIMIT_REQUESTS: int = 100\
    RATE_LIMIT_PERIOD: int = 60\
\
    # Monitoring\
    LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY: Optional[str] = None\
    LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY: Optional[str] = None\
    LANGFUSE_HOST: str = "https://cloud.langfuse.com"\
\
    # Vector\
    VECTOR_DIMENSION: int = 1536\
    EMBEDDING_MODEL: str = "text-embedding-3-small"\
\
    class Config:\
        env_file = ".env"\
        env_file_encoding = "utf-8"\
        # Protect against exposing sensitive values\
        env_prefix = ""\
        case_sensitive = False\
\
\
settings = Settings()\
EOF

# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Secure API Key Management\

cat > src/app/api/v1/chat.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ OpenAI-Compatible Chat Endpoint\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import List, Optional\
\
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException\
from pydantic import BaseModel\
import httpx\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
from app.core.secrets import get_openai_api_key, redact_sensitive_data\
\
router = APIRouter()\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Request/Response Models âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
class ChatMessage(BaseModel):\
    role: str\
    content: str\
\
class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):\
    model: str = "gpt-4o-mini"\
    messages: List[ChatMessage]\
    stream: bool = False\
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7\
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048\
\
class Choice(BaseModel):\
    index: int\
    message: ChatMessage\
    finish_reason: str\
\
class Usage(BaseModel):\
    prompt_tokens: int\
    completion_tokens: int\
    total_tokens: int\
\
class ChatCompletionResponse(BaseModel):\
    id: str\
    object: str\
    created: int\
    model: str\
    choices: List[Choice]\
    usage: Optional[Usage] = None\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Endpoint âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@router.post("/v1/chat/completions")\
async def chat_completions(request: ChatCompletionRequest):\
    """\
    OpenAI-compatible chat completion endpoint.\
    Uses secure API key management.\
    """\
    try:\
        # Get API key securely\
        api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
    except ValueError as e:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=503,\
            detail="OpenAI API key not configured. Please check server configuration."\
        )\
\
    # Prepare OpenAI API request\
    headers = {\
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",\
        "Content-Type": "application/json",\
    }\
\
    openai_payload = {\
        "model": request.model,\
        "messages": [msg.dict() for msg in request.messages],\
        "temperature": request.temperature,\
        "max_tokens": request.max_tokens,\
    }\
\
    if request.stream:\
        openai_payload["stream"] = True\
\
    try:\
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:\
            response = await client.post(\
                f"{settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions",\
                headers=headers,\
                json=openai_payload,\
            )\
\
        if response.status_code != 200:\
            # Redact any sensitive data from error response\
            error_text = redact_sensitive_data(response.text)\
            raise HTTPException(\
                status_code=response.status_code,\
                detail=f"OpenAI API error: {error_text}"\
            )\
\
        openai_response = response.json()\
\
        choices = []\
        for idx, choice in enumerate(openai_response.get("choices", [])):\
            choices.append(\
                Choice(\
                    index=idx,\
                    message=ChatMessage(\
                        role=choice.get("message", {}).get("role", "assistant"),\
                        content=choice.get("message", {}).get("content", ""),\
                    ),\
                    finish_reason=choice.get("finish_reason", "stop"),\
                )\
            )\
\
        usage = None\
        if "usage" in openai_response:\
            usage = Usage(\
                prompt_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("prompt_tokens", 0),\
                completion_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("completion_tokens", 0),\
                total_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("total_tokens", 0),\
            )\
\
        return ChatCompletionResponse(\
            id=openai_response.get("id", "chatcmpl-unknown"),\
            object=openai_response.get("object", "chat.completion"),\
            created=openai_response.get("created", 1234567890),\
            model=openai_response.get("model", request.model),\
            choices=choices,\
            usage=usage,\
        )\
\
    except httpx.TimeoutException:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=504,\
            detail="OpenAI API request timed out"\
        )\
    except Exception as e:\
        # Redact sensitive data from error\
        error_msg = redact_sensitive_data(str(e))\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=500,\
            detail=f"Error calling OpenAI API: {error_msg}"\
        )\
EOF
cat > scripts/test_openai_connection.py << 'EOF'\
#!/usr/bin/env python3\
# ============================================================\
# OpenAI API Key Test Script (Secure Version)\
# ============================================================\
# Securely tests API key from environment variables\
# Never logs or exposes the actual key\
# ============================================================\
\
import sys\
import time\
from datetime import datetime\
from typing import Optional\
\
import httpx\
from rich.console import Console\
from rich.table import Table\
from rich.panel import Panel\
from rich.progress import Progress, SpinnerColumn, TextColumn\
from rich import box\
\
# Add src to path for imports\
import sys\
from pathlib import Path\
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
from app.core.secrets import get_openai_api_key, redacted_key, SecretManager\
\
console = Console()\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Configuration âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\

def print_header():\
    """Print the test header."""\
    header = """\
âƒµƒ´âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ·\
âƒµƒ±                    OpenAI API Connection Test                   âƒµƒ±\
âƒµƒ±                      (Secure Version)                           âƒµƒ±\
âƒµƒºâƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ°âƒµƒ½\
"""\
    console.print(Panel(header, style="bold blue", box=box.DOUBLE))\

def test_api_key() -> bool:\
    """Test if the API key is valid."""\
    with Progress(\
        SpinnerColumn(),\
        TextColumn("[progress.description]{task.description}"),\
        console=console,\
    ) as progress:\
        task = progress.add_task("[cyan]Testing API key validity...", total=None)\
\
        try:\
            api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
            headers = {\
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",\
                "Content-Type": "application/json",\
            }\
\
            with httpx.Client(timeout=10.0) as client:\
                response = client.get(\
                    f"{settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL}/models",\
                    headers=headers,\
                )\
\
            progress.update(task, completed=True)\
\
            if response.status_code == 200:\
                console.print("âƒ¼ƒ¥ [green]API key is valid[/green]")\
                console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ± Key: [dim]{redacted_key()}[/dim]")\
                data = response.json()\
                model_count = len(data.get("data", []))\
                console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒª Available models: [bold]{model_count}[/bold]")\
                return True\
            elif response.status_code == 401:\
                console.print("âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Invalid API key[/red]")\
                console.print(f"   Status: {response.status_code}")\
                return False\
            else:\
                console.print(f"âƒºƒ€ï¸ƒ¯  [yellow]Unexpected response: {response.status_code}[/yellow]")\
                return False\
\
        except ValueError as e:\
            console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Configuration error: {str(e)}[/red]")\
            return False\
        except httpx.TimeoutException:\
            console.print("âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Connection timeout - API endpoint unreachable[/red]")\
            return False\
        except Exception as e:\
            console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Error: {str(e)}[/red]")\
            return False\
\
\
def test_chat_completion() -> Optional[dict]:\
    """Test a simple chat completion."""\
    with Progress(\
        SpinnerColumn(),\
        TextColumn("[progress.description]{task.description}"),\
        console=console,\
    ) as progress:\
        task = progress.add_task("[cyan]Testing chat completion...", total=None)\
\
        try:\
            api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
            headers = {\
                "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",\
                "Content-Type": "application/json",\
            }\
\
            payload = {\
                "model": TEST_MODEL,\
                "messages": [\
                    {"role": "user", "content": "Reply with exactly 'OK' if you can read this."}\
                ],\
                "max_tokens": 10,\
                "temperature": 0,\
            }\
\
            start_time = time.time()\
\
            with httpx.Client(timeout=30.0) as client:\
                response = client.post(\
                    f"{settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions",\
                    headers=headers,\
                    json=payload,\
                )\
\
            elapsed = time.time() - start_time\
            progress.update(task, completed=True)\
\
            if response.status_code == 200:\
                data = response.json()\
                console.print("âƒ¼ƒ¥ [green]Chat completion successful[/green]")\
                console.print(f"   âƒ¯±ï¸ƒ¯  Response time: [bold]{elapsed:.2f}[/bold] seconds")\
\
                choices = data.get("choices", [])\
                if choices:\
                    message = choices[0].get("message", {})\
                    content = message.get("content", "")\
                    console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ²¬ Response: [italic]\"{content}\"[/italic]")\
\
                usage = data.get("usage", {})\
                if usage:\
                    console.print(f"   ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ½ Tokens used: [bold]{usage.get('total_tokens', 0)}[/bold]")\
\
                return data\
            else:\
                console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Chat completion failed: {response.status_code}[/red]")\
                return None\
\
        except ValueError as e:\
            console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Configuration error: {str(e)}[/red]")\
            return None\
        except Exception as e:\
            console.print(f"âƒ½ƒ¬ [red]Error: {str(e)}[/red]")\
            return None\
\
\
def main():\
    """Run all tests."""\
    print_header()\
\
    # Show key status (redacted)\
    table = Table(title="Configuration Status", box=box.ROUNDED)\
    table.add_column("Property", style="cyan", no_wrap=True)\
    table.add_column("Value", style="white")\
\
    table.add_row("API Base URL", settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL)\
    table.add_row("Test Model", TEST_MODEL)\
\
    try:\
        api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
        table.add_row("API Key Status", "[green]âƒ¼ƒ³ Configured[/green]")\
        table.add_row("API Key (redacted)", redacted_key())\
    except ValueError:\
        table.add_row("API Key Status", "[red]âƒ¼ƒ· Missing or invalid[/red]")\
\
    table.add_row("Timestamp", datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC"))\
    console.print(table)\
    console.print()\

    console.print("[bold]ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ± Testing API Credentials[/bold]")\
    console.print("âƒ´€" * 60)\
    is_valid = test_api_key()\
    console.print()\
\
    if not is_valid:\
        console.print("[red]âƒ½ƒ¬ API key validation failed.[/red]")\
        console.print("[yellow]Please check your .env file: OPENAI_API_KEY=REDACTED
        sys.exit(1)\
\
    # Test chat completion\
    console.print("[bold]ðƒ¿ƒ²¬ Testing Chat Completion[/bold]")\
    console.print("âƒ´€" * 60)\
    result = test_chat_completion()\
    console.print()\
\
    if result:\
        console.print("[green]âƒ¼ƒ¥ All tests passed![/green]")\
        console.print("[dim]Your OpenAI API key is working correctly.[/dim]")\
        console.print()\
        console.print("[bold]ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒ« Summary:[/bold]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ API Key: [green]Valid[/green] (redacted: {redacted_key()})")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ Connection: [green]Successful[/green]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ Model: [green]{TEST_MODEL}[/green]")\
        console.print(f"   â€ƒ‚ Response: [green]Received[/green]")\
        sys.exit(0)\
    else:\
        console.print("[red]âƒ½ƒ¬ Tests failed. Please check your configuration.[/red]")\
        sys.exit(1)\
\
\
if __name__ == "__main__":\
    main()\

cat > src/app/api/v1/chat.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ OpenAI-Compatible Chat Endpoint\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import List, Optional\
\
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException\
from pydantic import BaseModel\
import httpx\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
from app.core.secrets import get_openai_api_key, redact_sensitive_data\
\
router = APIRouter()\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Request/Response Models âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
class ChatMessage(BaseModel):\
    role: str\
    content: str\
\
class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):\
    model: str = "gpt-4o-mini"  # Default to available model\
    messages: List[ChatMessage]\
    stream: bool = False\
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7\
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048\
\
class Choice(BaseModel):\
    index: int\
    message: ChatMessage\
    finish_reason: str\
\
class Usage(BaseModel):\
    prompt_tokens: int\
    completion_tokens: int\
    total_tokens: int\
\
class ChatCompletionResponse(BaseModel):\
    id: str\
    object: str\
    created: int\
    model: str\
    choices: List[Choice]\
    usage: Optional[Usage] = None\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Endpoint âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
@router.post("/v1/chat/completions")\
async def chat_completions(request: ChatCompletionRequest):\
    """\
    OpenAI-compatible chat completion endpoint.\
    Uses secure API key management.\
    """\
    try:\
        api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
    except ValueError as e:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=503,\
            detail="OpenAI API key not configured. Please check server configuration."\
        )\
\
    # Use the model from request or fallback to config\
    model_to_use = request.model or settings.OPENAI_MODEL or "gpt-4o-mini"\
\
    headers = {\
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",\
        "Content-Type": "application/json",\
    }\
\
    openai_payload = {\
        "model": model_to_use,\
        "messages": [msg.dict() for msg in request.messages],\
        "temperature": request.temperature,\
        "max_tokens": request.max_tokens,\
    }\
\
    if request.stream:\
        openai_payload["stream"] = True\
\
    try:\
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:\
            response = await client.post(\
                f"{settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions",\
                headers=headers,\
                json=openai_payload,\
            )\
\
        if response.status_code != 200:\
            error_text = redact_sensitive_data(response.text)\
            \
            # Provide helpful error for model not found\
            if "model_not_found" in error_text:\
                raise HTTPException(\
                    status_code=400,\
                    detail=f"Model '{model_to_use}' not found or not accessible. "\
                           f"Please check available models at https://platform.openai.com/account/limits"\
                )\
            \
            raise HTTPException(\
                status_code=response.status_code,\
                detail=f"OpenAI API error: {error_text}"\
            )\
\
        openai_response = response.json()\
\
        choices = []\
        for idx, choice in enumerate(openai_response.get("choices", [])):\
            choices.append(\
                Choice(\
                    index=idx,\
                    message=ChatMessage(\
                        role=choice.get("message", {}).get("role", "assistant"),\
                        content=choice.get("message", {}).get("content", ""),\
                    ),\
                    finish_reason=choice.get("finish_reason", "stop"),\
                )\
            )\
\
        usage = None\
        if "usage" in openai_response:\
            usage = Usage(\
                prompt_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("prompt_tokens", 0),\
                completion_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("completion_tokens", 0),\
                total_tokens=openai_response["usage"].get("total_tokens", 0),\
            )\
\
        return ChatCompletionResponse(\
            id=openai_response.get("id", "chatcmpl-unknown"),\
            object=openai_response.get("object", "chat.completion"),\
            created=openai_response.get("created", 1234567890),\
            model=openai_response.get("model", model_to_use),\
            choices=choices,\
            usage=usage,\
        )\
\
    except httpx.TimeoutException:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=504,\
            detail="OpenAI API request timed out"\
        )\
    except Exception as e:\
        error_msg = redact_sensitive_data(str(e))\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=500,\
            detail=f"Error calling OpenAI API: {error_msg}"\
        )\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ LangGraph Agent State\
# ============================================================\
\
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any, Annotated, TypedDict\
from langgraph.graph import add_messages\
\
\
class AgentState(TypedDict):\
    """State for the LangGraph agent."""\
    messages: Annotated[List[Dict[str, Any]], add_messages]\
    current_query: str\
    context: Optional[str]\
    reasoning_trace: List[str]\
    iteration: int\
    max_iterations: int\
    confidence_score: Optional[float]\
    final_response: Optional[str]\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ LangGraph Agent Graph\

import json\

\

\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ LLM Setup âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
def get_llm():\
    """Get the OpenAI LLM instance."""\
    api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
    return ChatOpenAI(\
        model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL,\
        api_key=api_key,\
        base_url=settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL,\
        temperature=0.7,\
    )\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Node Functions âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
def planner_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Plan the task and decompose the query."""\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    query = messages[-1].content if messages else ""\
\
    # Simple planning for now - in Phase 3 we'll add more complexity\
    plan = f"Analyzing query: {query}"\
    \
    return {\
        "current_query": query,\
        "reasoning_trace": [plan],\
        "iteration": state.get("iteration", 0) + 1,\
    }\
\
\
def retriever_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Retrieve relevant context (placeholder for Phase 3)."""\
    query = state.get("current_query", "")\
    \
    # Phase 3 will replace this with actual vector search\
    context = f"[Retrieved context for: {query}]\n"\
    context += "Currently in Phase 2: Agent loop without retrieval.\n"\
    context += "Phase 3 will add pgvector for document search."\
    \
    return {"context": context}\
\
\
def reasoner_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Generate a response using the LLM."""\
    llm = get_llm()\
    query = state.get("current_query", "")\
    context = state.get("context", "")\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    \
    # Build the prompt with context\
    prompt = f"""You are a helpful RAG assistant. Use the following context to answer the user's question.\
\
Context:\
{context}\
\
User question: {query}\
\
If the context doesn't contain the answer, say "I don't have enough information to answer that" and provide general information if possible.\
"""\
    \
    response = llm.invoke(prompt)\
    \
    return {\
        "messages": [AIMessage(content=response.content)],\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + ["Generated response using context"]\
    }\
\
\
def verifier_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Verify the response quality (placeholder for Phase 3)."""\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    response = messages[-1].content if messages else ""\
    \
    # Simple confidence check based on length\
    confidence_score = min(1.0, len(response) / 100)\
    \
    # Check for "don't have enough information" pattern\
    if "don't have enough information" in response or "not enough information" in response:\
        confidence_score = max(0.3, confidence_score * 0.5)\
    \
    return {\
        "confidence_score": confidence_score,\
        "final_response": response if confidence_score > 0.3 else "I need more context to give a confident answer."\
    }\
\
\
def should_continue(state: AgentState) -> str:\
    """Determine if the agent should continue or end."""\
    # If confidence is high enough or we've done enough iterations\
    confidence = state.get("confidence_score", 0)\
    iteration = state.get("iteration", 0)\
    max_iterations = state.get("max_iterations", 2)\
    \
    if confidence > 0.7:\
        return "synthesize"\
    elif iteration >= max_iterations:\
        return "synthesize"\
    else:\
        return "reasoner"\
\
\
def synthesizer_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Synthesize the final response."""\
    final_response = state.get("final_response", "No response generated.")\
    \
    return {\
        "messages": [AIMessage(content=final_response)],\
        "final_response": final_response,\
    }\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Build the Graph âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
def build_agent_graph():\
    """Build and compile the LangGraph agent."""\
    # Create the graph\
    builder = StateGraph(AgentState)\
\
    # Add nodes\
    builder.add_node("planner", planner_node)\
    builder.add_node("retriever", retriever_node)\
    builder.add_node("reasoner", reasoner_node)\
    builder.add_node("verifier", verifier_node)\
    builder.add_node("synthesizer", synthesizer_node)\
\
    # Add edges\
    builder.set_entry_point("planner")\
    builder.add_edge("planner", "retriever")\
    builder.add_edge("retriever", "reasoner")\
    builder.add_edge("reasoner", "verifier")\
    \
    # Conditional edges\
    builder.add_conditional_edges(\
        "verifier",\
        should_continue,\
        {\
            "reasoner": "reasoner",\
            "synthesize": "synthesizer",\
        }\
    )\
    builder.add_edge("synthesizer", END)\
\
    # Compile with memory\
    memory = MemorySaver()\
    graph = builder.compile(checkpointer=memory)\
    \
    return graph\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Singleton graph instance âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
_agent_graph = None\
\
def get_agent_graph():\
    """Get or create the agent graph singleton."""\
    global _agent_graph\
    if _agent_graph is None:\
        _agent_graph = build_agent_graph()\
    return _agent_graph\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ Agent Service\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import AsyncGenerator, Dict, Any, List\
\
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, AIMessage\
\
from app.services.agent.graph import get_agent_graph\
from app.services.agent.state import AgentState\
\
\
class AgentService:\
    """Service for interacting with the LangGraph agent."""\
\
    def __init__(self):\
        self.graph = get_agent_graph()\
\
    async def stream_chat(\
        self,\
        query: str,\
        conversation_id: str,\
        stream: bool = True,\
    ) -> AsyncGenerator[str, None]:\
        """\
        Process a chat query through the LangGraph agent.\
        Yields NDJSON messages for streaming.\
        """\
        # Prepare initial state\
        initial_state: AgentState = {\
            "messages": [HumanMessage(content=query)],\
            "current_query": query,\
            "context": None,\
            "reasoning_trace": [],\
            "iteration": 0,\
            "max_iterations": 2,\
            "confidence_score": None,\
            "final_response": None,\
        }\
\
        # Configuration for the graph execution\
        config = {\
            "configurable": {\
                "thread_id": conversation_id,\
            }\
        }\
\
        # Run the graph\
        final_state = None\
        async for event in self.graph.astream(\
            initial_state,\
            config=config,\
            stream_mode="values",\
        ):\
            final_state = event\
            \
            # Extract response if available\
            messages = event.get("messages", [])\
            if messages and isinstance(messages[-1], AIMessage):\
                response = messages[-1].content\
                \
                # Yield the thinking trace\
                trace = event.get("reasoning_trace", [])\
                if trace:\
                    yield json.dumps({\
                        "type": "thinking",\
                        "data": trace[-1] if trace else "",\
                    }) + "\n"\
                \
                # Yield the token (full response for now)\
                yield json.dumps({\
                    "type": "token",\
                    "data": response,\
                }) + "\n"\
\
        # Done\
        if final_state and final_state.get("final_response"):\
            yield json.dumps({\
                "type": "done",\
                "data": final_state.get("final_response"),\
            }) + "\n"\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Singleton service âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
_agent_service = None\
\
def get_agent_service():\
    """Get or create the agent service singleton."""\
    global _agent_service\
    if _agent_service is None:\
        _agent_service = AgentService()\
    return _agent_service\
EOF

# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ OpenAI-Compatible Chat Endpoint\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import List, Optional\
\
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request\
from pydantic import BaseModel\
import httpx\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
from app.core.secrets import get_openai_api_key, redact_sensitive_data\
from app.services.agent.service import get_agent_service\
\
router = APIRouter()\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Request/Response Models âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
class ChatMessage(BaseModel):\
    role: str\
    content: str\
\
class ChatCompletionRequest(BaseModel):\
    model: str = "gpt-4o-mini"\
    messages: List[ChatMessage]\
    stream: bool = False\
    temperature: Optional[float] = 0.7\
    max_tokens: Optional[int] = 2048\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Endpoint âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
@router.post("/v1/chat/completions")\
async def chat_completions(request: ChatCompletionRequest, req: Request):\
    """\
    OpenAI-compatible chat completion endpoint.\
    Uses LangGraph agent for intelligent responses.\
    """\
    # Extract the last user message\
    user_query = None\
    for msg in reversed(request.messages):\
        if msg.role == "user":\
            user_query = msg.content\
            break\
    \
    if not user_query:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=400,\
            detail="No user message found in request."\
        )\
\
    # If streaming is requested, use the agent with streaming\
    if request.stream:\
        from fastapi.responses import StreamingResponse\
        \
        # Use streaming mode\
        agent_service = get_agent_service()\
        \
        async def generate():\
            async for chunk in agent_service.stream_chat(\
                query=user_query,\
                conversation_id=str(req.client.host) if req.client else "anonymous",\
                stream=True,\
            ):\
                yield chunk\
        \
        return StreamingResponse(\
            generate(),\
            media_type="application/x-ndjson",\
            headers={\
                "Cache-Control": "no-cache",\
                "Connection": "keep-alive",\
                "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",\
            }\
        )\
    \
    # Non-streaming mode: use simple OpenAI proxy\
    try:\
        api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
    except ValueError as e:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=503,\
            detail="OpenAI API key not configured."\
        )\
\
    model_to_use = request.model or settings.OPENAI_MODEL or "gpt-4o-mini"\
\
    headers = {\
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",\
        "Content-Type": "application/json",\
    }\
\
    openai_payload = {\
        "model": model_to_use,\
        "messages": [msg.dict() for msg in request.messages],\
        "temperature": request.temperature,\
        "max_tokens": request.max_tokens,\
        "stream": False,\
    }\
\
    try:\
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=60.0) as client:\
            response = await client.post(\
                f"{settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL}/chat/completions",\
                headers=headers,\
                json=openai_payload,\
            )\
\
        if response.status_code != 200:\
            error_text = redact_sensitive_data(response.text)\
            if "model_not_found" in error_text:\
                raise HTTPException(\
                    status_code=400,\
                    detail=f"Model '{model_to_use}' not found or not accessible."\
                )\
            raise HTTPException(\
                status_code=response.status_code,\
                detail=f"OpenAI API error: {error_text}"\
            )\
\
        return response.json()\
\
    except httpx.TimeoutException:\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=504,\
            detail="OpenAI API request timed out"\
        )\
    except Exception as e:\
        error_msg = redact_sensitive_data(str(e))\
        raise HTTPException(\
            status_code=500,\
            detail=f"Error calling OpenAI API: {error_msg}"\
        )\
EOF
