cat > src/app/services/agent/graph.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ LangGraph Agent Graph\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional\
\
from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END\
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI\
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, AIMessage\
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
from app.core.secrets import get_openai_api_key\
from app.services.agent.state import AgentState\
from app.services.retrieval.service import get_retrieval_service\
from app.db.session import SessionLocal\
\
# Try different import paths for MemorySaver (compatibility)\
try:\
    from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import MemorySaver\
except ImportError:\
    try:\
        from langgraph.checkpoint import MemorySaver\
    except ImportError:\
        # Fallback: use a simple in-memory checkpoint\
        from langgraph.checkpoint import BaseCheckpointSaver\
        class MemorySaver(BaseCheckpointSaver):\
            def __init__(self):\
                self.storage = {}\
            def get(self, config):\
                return self.storage.get(config.get("configurable", {}).get("thread_id"))\
            def put(self, config, checkpoint):\
                self.storage[config.get("configurable", {}).get("thread_id")] = checkpoint\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ LLM Setup âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
def get_llm():\
    """Get the OpenAI LLM instance."""\
    api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
    return ChatOpenAI(\
        model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL,\
        api_key=api_key,\
        base_url=settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL,\
        temperature=0.7,\
    )\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Node Functions âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
def planner_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Plan the task and decompose the query."""\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    query = messages[-1].content if messages else ""\
\
    plan = f"ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ­ Analyzing query: {query}"\
    \
    return {\
        "current_query": query,\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + [plan],\
        "iteration": state.get("iteration", 0) + 1,\
    }\
\
\
async def retriever_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Retrieve relevant context from the vector database."""\
    query = state.get("current_query", "")\
    \
    db = SessionLocal()\
    try:\
        retrieval_service = get_retrieval_service(db)\
        results = await retrieval_service.search(query, top_k=5, min_score=0.3)\
        context = await retrieval_service.format_context(results)\
        \
        trace_msg = f"ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒº Retrieved {len(results)} relevant chunks"\
        if results:\
            trace_msg += f" (best score: {results[0]['similarity']:.2f})"\
        \
        return {\
            "context": context,\
            "retrieved_chunks": results,\
            "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + [trace_msg],\
        }\
    finally:\
        db.close()\
\
\
def reasoner_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Generate a response using the LLM with the retrieved context."""\
    llm = get_llm()\
    query = state.get("current_query", "")\
    context = state.get("context", "")\
    \
    prompt = f"""You are a helpful RAG assistant with access to a knowledge base.\
\
CONTEXT FROM KNOWLEDGE BASE:\
{context}\
\
USER QUESTION: {query}\
\
INSTRUCTIONS:\
1. Answer based ONLY on the provided context.\
2. If the context doesn't contain the answer, say "I don't have enough information to answer this question."\
3. Cite which source document the information comes from when possible.\
4. Be concise, clear, and factual.\
\
Generate your response:"""\
    \
    response = llm.invoke(prompt)\
    \
    return {\
        "messages": [AIMessage(content=response.content)],\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + ["ðƒ¿ƒ²ƒ Generated response using retrieved context"]\
    }\
\
\
def verifier_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Verify the response quality."""\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    response = messages[-1].content if messages else ""\
    \
    has_answer = "don't have enough information" not in response.lower()\
    has_sufficient_length = len(response) > 10\
    \
    confidence_score = 0.8 if has_answer and has_sufficient_length else 0.3\
    \
    return {\
        "confidence_score": confidence_score,\
        "final_response": response if confidence_score > 0.3 else "I need more context to give a confident answer."\
    }\
\
\
def should_continue(state: AgentState) -> str:\
    """Determine if the agent should continue or end."""\
    confidence = state.get("confidence_score", 0)\
    iteration = state.get("iteration", 0)\
    max_iterations = state.get("max_iterations", 2)\
    \
    if confidence > 0.7:\
        return "synthesize"\
    elif iteration >= max_iterations:\
        return "synthesize"\
    else:\
        return "reasoner"\
\
\
def synthesizer_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Synthesize the final response."""\
    final_response = state.get("final_response", "No response generated.")\
    \
    return {\
        "messages": [AIMessage(content=final_response)],\
        "final_response": final_response,\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + ["âƒ¼ƒ¥ Final response synthesized"]\
    }\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Build the Graph âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
def build_agent_graph():\
    """Build and compile the LangGraph agent."""\
    builder = StateGraph(AgentState)\
\
    builder.add_node("planner", planner_node)\
    builder.add_node("retriever", retriever_node)\
    builder.add_node("reasoner", reasoner_node)\
    builder.add_node("verifier", verifier_node)\
    builder.add_node("synthesizer", synthesizer_node)\
\
    builder.set_entry_point("planner")\
    builder.add_edge("planner", "retriever")\
    builder.add_edge("retriever", "reasoner")\
    builder.add_edge("reasoner", "verifier")\
    \
    builder.add_conditional_edges(\
        "verifier",\
        should_continue,\
        {\
            "reasoner": "reasoner",\
            "synthesize": "synthesizer",\
        }\
    )\
    builder.add_edge("synthesizer", END)\
\
    # Use MemorySaver for checkpointing\
    memory = MemorySaver()\
    graph = builder.compile(checkpointer=memory)\
    \
    return graph\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Singleton graph instance âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
_agent_graph = None\
\
def get_agent_graph():\
    global _agent_graph\
    if _agent_graph is None:\
        _agent_graph = build_agent_graph()\
    return _agent_graph\
EOF
cat > src/app/services/agent/state.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ LangGraph Agent State\
# ============================================================\
\
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any, Annotated, TypedDict\
from langgraph.graph.message import add_messages\
\
\
class AgentState(TypedDict):\
    """State for the LangGraph agent."""\
    messages: Annotated[List[Dict[str, Any]], add_messages]\
    current_query: str\
    context: Optional[str]\
    retrieved_chunks: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]\
    reasoning_trace: List[str]\
    iteration: int\
    max_iterations: int\
    confidence_score: Optional[float]\
    final_response: Optional[str]\
EOF

EOF

    builder.add_edge("retriever", "reasoner")\
    builder.add_edge("reasoner", "verifier")\

    builder.add_conditional_edges(\
        "verifier",\
        should_continue,\
        {\
            "reasoner": "reasoner",\
            "synthesize": "synthesizer",\
        }\
    )\
    builder.add_edge("synthesizer", END)\
\
    # Use MemorySaver for checkpointing\
    memory = MemorySaver()\
    graph = builder.compile(checkpointer=memory)\
    \
    return graph\

    """State for the LangGraph agent."""\
    messages: Annotated[List[Dict[str, Any]], add_messages]\

cat > src/app/services/agent/graph.py << 'EOF'\
# ============================================================\
# RAG Agent Platform â€ƒ´ LangGraph Agent Graph\
# ============================================================\
\
import json\
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional\
\
from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END\
from langchain_openai import ChatOpenAI\
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, AIMessage\
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session\
\
from app.core.config import settings\
from app.core.secrets import get_openai_api_key\
from app.services.agent.state import AgentState\
from app.services.retrieval.service import get_retrieval_service\
from app.db.session import SessionLocal\
\
# Try different import paths for MemorySaver (compatibility)\
try:\
    from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import MemorySaver\
except ImportError:\
    try:\
        from langgraph.checkpoint import MemorySaver\
    except ImportError:\
        # Fallback: simple in-memory checkpoint\
        class MemorySaver:\
            def __init__(self):\
                self.storage = {}\
            def get(self, config):\
                thread_id = config.get("configurable", {}).get("thread_id")\
                return self.storage.get(thread_id)\
            def put(self, config, checkpoint):\
                thread_id = config.get("configurable", {}).get("thread_id")\
                self.storage[thread_id] = checkpoint\
            def save_checkpoint(self, config, checkpoint, metadata):\
                self.put(config, checkpoint)\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ LLM Setup âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
def get_llm():\
    """Get the OpenAI LLM instance."""\
    api_key = get_openai_api_key()\
    return ChatOpenAI(\
        model=settings.OPENAI_MODEL,\
        api_key=api_key,\
        base_url=settings.OPENAI_BASE_URL,\
        temperature=0.7,\
    )\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Node Functions âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
def planner_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Plan the task and decompose the query."""\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    query = messages[-1].content if messages else ""\
\
    plan = f"ðƒ¿ƒ´ƒ­ Analyzing query: {query}"\
    \
    return {\
        "current_query": query,\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + [plan],\
        "iteration": state.get("iteration", 0) + 1,\
    }\
\
\
async def retriever_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Retrieve relevant context from the vector database."""\
    query = state.get("current_query", "")\
    \
    db = SessionLocal()\
    try:\
        retrieval_service = get_retrieval_service(db)\
        results = await retrieval_service.search(query, top_k=5, min_score=0.3)\
        context = await retrieval_service.format_context(results)\
        \
        trace_msg = f"ðƒ¿ƒ³ƒº Retrieved {len(results)} relevant chunks"\
        if results:\
            trace_msg += f" (best score: {results[0]['similarity']:.2f})"\
        \
        return {\
            "context": context,\
            "retrieved_chunks": results,\
            "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + [trace_msg],\
        }\
    finally:\
        db.close()\
\
\
def reasoner_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Generate a response using the LLM with the retrieved context."""\
    llm = get_llm()\
    query = state.get("current_query", "")\
    context = state.get("context", "")\
    \
    prompt = f"""You are a helpful RAG assistant with access to a knowledge base.\
\
CONTEXT FROM KNOWLEDGE BASE:\
{context}\
\
USER QUESTION: {query}\
\
INSTRUCTIONS:\
1. Answer based ONLY on the provided context.\
2. If the context doesn't contain the answer, say "I don't have enough information to answer this question."\
3. Cite which source document the information comes from when possible.\
4. Be concise, clear, and factual.\
\
Generate your response:"""\
    \
    response = llm.invoke(prompt)\
    \
    return {\
        "messages": [AIMessage(content=response.content)],\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + ["ðƒ¿ƒ²ƒ Generated response using retrieved context"]\
    }\
\
\
def verifier_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Verify the response quality."""\
    messages = state.get("messages", [])\
    response = messages[-1].content if messages else ""\
    \
    has_answer = "don't have enough information" not in response.lower()\
    has_sufficient_length = len(response) > 10\
    \
    confidence_score = 0.8 if has_answer and has_sufficient_length else 0.3\
    \
    return {\
        "confidence_score": confidence_score,\
        "final_response": response if confidence_score > 0.3 else "I need more context to give a confident answer."\
    }\
\
\
def should_continue(state: AgentState) -> str:\
    """Determine if the agent should continue or end."""\
    confidence = state.get("confidence_score", 0)\
    iteration = state.get("iteration", 0)\
    max_iterations = state.get("max_iterations", 2)\
    \
    if confidence > 0.7:\
        return "synthesize"\
    elif iteration >= max_iterations:\
        return "synthesize"\
    else:\
        return "reasoner"\
\
\
def synthesizer_node(state: AgentState) -> Dict[str, Any]:\
    """Synthesize the final response."""\
    final_response = state.get("final_response", "No response generated.")\
    \
    return {\
        "messages": [AIMessage(content=final_response)],\
        "final_response": final_response,\
        "reasoning_trace": state.get("reasoning_trace", []) + ["âƒ¼ƒ¥ Final response synthesized"]\
    }\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Build the Graph âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
\
def build_agent_graph():\
    """Build and compile the LangGraph agent."""\
    builder = StateGraph(AgentState)\
\
    builder.add_node("planner", planner_node)\
    builder.add_node("retriever", retriever_node)\
    builder.add_node("reasoner", reasoner_node)\
    builder.add_node("verifier", verifier_node)\
    builder.add_node("synthesizer", synthesizer_node)\
\
    builder.set_entry_point("planner")\
    builder.add_edge("planner", "retriever")\
    builder.add_edge("retriever", "reasoner")\
    builder.add_edge("reasoner", "verifier")\
    \
    builder.add_conditional_edges(\
        "verifier",\
        should_continue,\
        {\
            "reasoner": "reasoner",\
            "synthesize": "synthesizer",\
        }\
    )\
    builder.add_edge("synthesizer", END)\
\
    # Use MemorySaver for checkpointing\
    memory = MemorySaver()\
    graph = builder.compile(checkpointer=memory)\
    \
    return graph\
\
\
# âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€ Singleton graph instance âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€âƒ´€\
_agent_graph = None\
\
def get_agent_graph():\
    global _agent_graph\
    if _agent_graph is None:\
        _agent_graph = build_agent_graph()\
    return _agent_graph\
EOF

cat > src/app/main.py << 'EOF'\
import sys\
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager\
from fastapi import FastAPI\
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\
\
from app.api.router import api_router\
from app.core.security import print_startup_banner\
\
\
@asynccontextmanager\
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\
    sys.stdout.write("ðƒ¿ƒº€ Starting lifespan...\n")\
    sys.stdout.flush()\
    access_code = print_startup_banner()\
    app.state.access_code = access_code\
    yield\
    sys.stdout.write("ðƒ¿ƒ»ƒ± Shutting down...\n")\
    sys.stdout.flush()\
\
\
app = FastAPI(\
    title="RAG Agent Platform",\
    version="0.1.0",\
    description="Production-ready RAG agent with LangGraph, pgvector, and FastAPI",\
    lifespan=lifespan,\
)\
\
app.add_middleware(\
    CORSMiddleware,\
    allow_origins=["*"],\
    allow_credentials=True,\
    allow_methods=["*"],\
    allow_headers=["*"],\
)\
\
# Mount the API router at /api\
app.include_router(api_router, prefix="/api")\
\
@app.get("/health")\
async def health_check():\
    return {"status": "ok", "service": "RAG Agent Platform"}\
\
@app.get("/")\
async def root():\
    return {\
        "message": "RAG Agent Platform API",\
        "docs": "/docs",\
        "health": "/health",\
    }\
EOF
